2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - Vivado 2018.3, XC7A100T-FGG484-2 (xc7a100tfgg484-2), single 50 MHz clock domain.
   - GCC-PHAT 4-mic direction finding: outputs `target_azimuth_01deg` (signed 16-bit, ±1800 = ±180.0°), `target_elevation_01deg` (0..900 = 0..90.0°), `direction_valid` single-cycle pulse (~39 Hz).
   - SG90: 20 ms (50 Hz) period, 0.5–2.5 ms high pulse = 0–180° (some clones 1–2 ms; center may be ~1.52 ms); analog servo needs continuous PWM; 4.8–6 V power, peak ~0.5–0.7 A each; 3.3 V signal usually acceptable.
   - Pulse width math @50 MHz: 20 ms = 1,000,000 cycles; 0.5 ms = 25,000; 2.5 ms = 125,000; `pulse = PULSE_MIN + angle_01deg * (PULSE_MAX−PULSE_MIN) / 1800`.
   - Mapping: az≥0 → pan=az, tilt=el; az<0 → pan=az+180°, tilt=180°−el; pan clamp 0..1800; tilt clamp 100..1800 (10..180°).
   - Period-boundary pulse-width loading for glitch-free PWM; 2-cycle compute pipeline after `update`.
   - Vivado pin verification: `link_design -part xc7a100tfgg484-2`, `get_package_pins`; C18=IO_L13P_T2_MRCC_16, C19=IO_L13N_T2_MRCC_16, both Bank 16 (LVCMOS33).
   - SystemVerilog testbench with parameter-scaled PWM period for fast simulation; negedge-driven stimulus to avoid race conditions.

3. Files and Code Sections:

     - Key combinational mapping:
       ```systemverilog
       always @(*) begin
           if (az_01deg >= 16'sd0) begin
               pan_raw  = az_01deg;
               tilt_raw = el_01deg;
           end else begin
               pan_raw  = az_01deg + 32'sd1800;
               tilt_raw = 32'sd1800 - el_01deg;
           end
           pan_raw  = pan_raw  + PAN_TRIM_01DEG;
           tilt_raw = tilt_raw + TILT_TRIM_01DEG;
           if (pan_raw < 0)         pan_clamp = 0;
           else if (pan_raw > 1800) pan_clamp = 1800;
           else                     pan_clamp = pan_raw;
           if (tilt_raw < TILT_MIN_01DEG) tilt_clamp = TILT_MIN_01DEG;
           else if (tilt_raw > 1800)      tilt_clamp = 1800;
           else                           tilt_clamp = tilt_raw;
       end
       ```
     - Update pipeline (calc_d delayed one cycle, computes `pan_hi_tgt <= PULSE_MIN_CYC + (pan_ang_r * SPAN_CYC) / 1800;`), init pose pan 90°/tilt 90°.
     - PWM timebase: `period_cnt` 20-bit, compares `period_cnt == PWM_PERIOD_CYC - 1`, loads `pan_hi_act/tilt_hi_act` at boundary; `pan_pwm <= (period_cnt < pan_hi_act);`.

   - Vote window: 128 frames = 3.28 s before vote_ready/first valid output; old SEARCH_PER=4 → angle target refresh 102.4 ms; changed to SEARCH_PER=1 → 25.6 ms refresh (solver uses previous frame's vote, so effective command latency ~50–70 ms + servo).
   - Real-recording TBs (tb_i2s_multi etc.) use only 15 frames and predate/are incompatible with 128-frame voting semantics; not suitable for validating window changes.
   - Batch regression run_sim_all.sh counts lines containing PASS/FAIL/ERROR; TBs must not print literal "FAIL" on success.

Summary:
1. Primary Request and Intent:
   - Original project: read `doc/`, integrate two SG90 servos as a 2D gimbal controlled by standard 20 ms PWM. Azimuth servo on FPGA pin C19, elevation servo on C18. Servos accept 0–180°, so when azimuth is −180°…0°, the gimbal must flip: (az, el) = (−150°, +30°) → (pan, tilt) = (30°, 150°). Due to mechanics, servo #2 (tilt) accepts only 10°–180°.
   - Earlier explicit instruction: verify SG90 driving method online and proactively flag problems in the user's commands. This was done in prior work (20 ms period, 0.5–2.5 ms standard, some clones 1–2 ms, external 5 V supply/common ground, analog servo needs continuous PWM, ~0.1 s/60°).
   - Earlier request: "根据时序来看，还有大量冗余，能不能加速一下角度的识别和结算" — use timing slack to speed angle recognition/solving.
   - Latest explicit request: "64 帧提前锁定加50% 重叠帧" — implement both:
     1. Early vote lock at 64 frames instead of 128.
     2. 50% overlapping frames: 256-point window, hop = 128 samples, new frame every 12.8 ms (78.125 fps).
   - Deliverables expected: working RTL, updated simulations, rebuilt bitstream, updated docs/README, and a report of results.

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - Vivado 2018.3, XC7A100T-FGG484-2 (`xc7a100tfgg484-2`), single 50 MHz clock domain.
   - GCC-PHAT 4-mic direction finding: 10 kHz I2S (BCLK 640 kHz), 256-point frames; 4 parallel 256-point FFTs; 6-pair PHAT/IFFT (1024-point zero-padded interpolation); joint 128-frame histogram voting; outputs `target_azimuth_01deg` (±1800) and `target_elevation_01deg` (0..900), `direction_valid` pulse.
   - Servo mapping: az≥0 → pan=az, tilt=el; az<0 → pan=az+180°, tilt=180°−el; pan clamp 0..1800; tilt clamp 100..1800; 20 ms = 1,000,000 cycles @50 MHz; pulse 0.5–2.5 ms = 25,000–125,000 cycles; widths loaded at PWM period boundary; init 90°/90°.
   - 50% overlap: first frame still needs 25.6 ms to fill 256 samples; thereafter a new 256-sample window every 128 samples = 12.8 ms (78.125 fps).
   - Sliding buffer: depth 512 per channel, 9-bit circular write pointer; `rd_base` snapshots window start each `frame_valid`; read address = `rd_base + rd_addr` mod 512. A 256-cycle read (~5 µs) is far shorter than the 12.8 ms needed for writes to reach the window start, so one buffer is safe without ping-pong.
   - AGC must remain on a 256-sample cadence (`agc_frame_done`), decoupled from the 128-sample frame hop, to avoid gain jitter/mask re-learning.
   - Vote pipeline: vote FSM updates `tau*_vote` in `V_LATCH`; main solver at frame N uses the previous frame's vote. Thus first `direction_valid` is one frame after `vote_ready`.
   - Precise timing: 3 mask-learning frames + 64 voted frames + 1 solver pipeline frame → first valid at about 0.88 s (frame 68 at 12.8 ms hop). Full 128-frame vote window ≈1.64 s of audio, correlated because of overlap (effective independent averaging roughly half of non-overlapped 3.28 s).
   - Previous build (before overlap/early lock) results: WNS +2.024 ns, WHS +0.013 ns, TNS/THS 0; LUT 37983 (59.91%), FF 43332 (34.17%), Slice 12562 (79.26%), BRAM 29.5, DSP 192 (80%), IOB 18; DRC 0 Error, 446 warnings/advisories; bit 2,950,143 B SHA256 `05a4c544f5448649b0eda9019ed8561d1d62d3fee3e5cbc0781a840faaed5a6a`; bin 2,950,020 B SHA256 `4255e12260c171c11621c6f77891d887f36bf8286bb67047dceb7d72aef0b2ee`; timestamp 2026-11-01 19:35.
   - The hold margin was only +0.013 ns, so any RTL change requires a fresh implementation/timing check.

1. Primary Request and Intent:
   - Original project intent (earlier context): read `doc/`, integrate two SG90 servos as a 2D gimbal with standard 20 ms PWM; azimuth on pin C19, elevation on C18; flip gimbal when azimuth is negative ((−150°, +30°) → pan 30°, tilt 150°); tilt servo mechanically limited to 10°–180°.
   - Earlier explicit requests: verify SG90 driving online and proactively flag problems; use timing slack to accelerate angle recognition/solving.
   - Previous explicit request: "64 帧提前锁定加50% 重叠帧" — implemented early vote lock at 64 frames and 50% overlapping frames (hop=128, 12.8 ms/frame).
   - **Latest explicit user request (the active task): "还是无法实时显示，感觉与实际相比延迟了1s左右"** — the display is still not real-time, lagging about 1 s behind reality. This must be diagnosed and fixed in RTL, verified in simulation, rebuilt into a bitstream, and documented.
   - Deliverables expected: working RTL, updated testbenches/simulations, rebuilt bitstream, updated docs/README, and a final report.

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - Vivado 2018.3, XC7A100T-FGG484-2 (`xc7a100tfgg484-2`), single 50 MHz clock, 20 ns period.
   - GCC-PHAT 4-mic direction finding: 10 kHz I2S (BCLK 640 kHz), 256-point windows; 4 parallel 256-point FFTs; 6-pair PHAT/IFFT (1024-point zero-padded); joint 128-frame histogram voting; outputs `target_azimuth_01deg` (±1800) and `target_elevation_01deg` (0..900), `direction_valid` pulse.
   - 50% overlap: first window 25.6 ms, then new 256-sample window every 128 samples (12.8 ms, 78.125 fps); 512-deep circular buffer with `rd_base` snapshot; AGC still per 256 samples (`agc_frame_done`).
   - Vote pipeline: 6 pair histograms × 23 bins (±11 samples, 1 sample/bin); 128-entry ring buffer of bins; 23×23 joint search each frame; parabolic interpolation; 3-point median output; confidence `best_score > filled`; `VOTE_LOCK_FILL=64` early lock.
   - **Root cause of ~1 s lag**: 128-frame sliding window peak flips only when new-direction votes exceed old (~64 frames × 12.8 ms ≈ 0.82 s).
   - **Fast re-lock design**: 5-point median τ (`fb01/fb23`) tracked continuously; when six pairs all valid and `vote_ready`, 12 consecutive frames with |bin(fb) − best_bin| ≥ 2 bins → clear histograms and re-accumulate; `vote_ready` stays 1; output switches immediately to fb values; steady state still 128-frame vote.
   - Timing: first build of re-lock logic caused WNS −0.492 ns (`t23_h3 → fast_cnt`, 28 levels, 20.364 ns); fixed by registering `fb01/fb23` (1 clock), giving WNS +0.664 ns.
   - `med5`: 9-comparator sorting network of 16-bit signed τ; `bin_of` maps τ to bin with offset 11776 (=11.5×1024).
   - UART 91-byte frame every 12.8 ms at 78 fps; segment display 78 Hz; servo PWM 50 Hz (20 ms, 1,000,000 cycles; 0.5–2.5 ms = 25,000–125,000 cycles).

3. Files and Code Sections:

       wire signed [15:0] raw01 = $signed(tdoa_result[0]);
       wire signed [15:0] raw23 = $signed(tdoa_result[5]);
       wire signed [15:0] med01 = med5(raw01, t01_h0, t01_h1, t01_h2, t01_h3);
       wire signed [15:0] med23 = med5(raw23, t23_h0, t23_h1, t23_h2, t23_h3);
       wire signed [15:0] fb01 = (filt_cnt < 3'd4) ? raw01 : med01;
       wire signed [15:0] fb23 = (filt_cnt < 3'd4) ? raw23 : med23;
       ```

       lo

# Data Visualization

A chart is **read by people and executed by you**. This skill turns "make it look
good" into a procedure with checks, so the result is right by construction rather
than by taste.

**The method here is design-system-agnostic.** Nothing in the procedure, the form
heuristic, the six checks, or the mark specs is specific to one product. A design
system supplies a small set of *parameters* (its ramps, a categorical order, a
diverging pair, a status palette, a texture, its surfaces, its filter components);
the method consumes them unchanged. A **validated default palette** is the
reference instance, fully specified in `references/palette.md`. To target your
brand, read that file's structure and substitute its values - touch nothing else.

> The single most important habit: **the color part is computable, so compute it.**
> Never eyeball whether a palette is colorblind-safe - run `scripts/validate_palette.js`.

## The procedure - do these in order

Color comes LAST. Most bad charts pick colors first.

1. **Pick the form.** What is the data's job - magnitude, identity, polarity, a
   single headline, change-over-time? The job picks the chart type, and sometimes
   the answer is *not a chart* (a stat tile or hero number). -> `references/choosing-a-form.md`
2. **Assign color by the job it does.** Categorical (identity), sequential
   (magnitude), diverging (polarity), or status (state) - each has one rule.
   Assign categorical hues in fixed order, never cycled. -> `references/color-formula.md`
3. **VALIDATE the palette - run the script, don't reason about Delta E.**
   `node scripts/validate_palette.js "<hex,hex,...>" --mode light` (relative to
   this skill's base directory - or load it as `<script type="module">` in the
   chart's own page, where it reads
   `data-palette` off `<body>` and logs a `console.table` report). It returns
   pass/fail on the lightness band, chroma floor, adjacent-pair CVD separation,
   the normal-vision floor, and contrast. Fix anything that FAILs before continuing. Re-run for
   `--mode dark` with that mode's surface.
4. **Apply mark specs & spacers.** Thin marks, 4px rounded data-ends anchored to
   the baseline, 2px lines, >=8px markers, a 2px surface gap between fills (stacked
   segments and adjacent bars alike) and a 2px surface ring on overlapping marks,
   selective direct labels. -> `references/marks-and-anatomy.md`
5. **Add the hover layer - by default.** An HTML/SVG chart *is* interactive; ship
   a crosshair+tooltip on line/area and a per-mark hover tooltip on bar/dot/cell.
   The only form that skips it is a bare stat tile with no plot. Hit targets bigger
   than the mark; filters in one row above the charts. -> `references/interaction.md`
6. **Final accessibility pass.** For >= 2 series a legend is always present and <= 4
   are also direct-labeled (a single series needs no legend box - the title names
   it), so identity is never color-alone; a table view exists; dark mode is **selected** - its own
   steps from the same ramps, validated against the dark surface, not an automatic
   flip; texture is available for the CVD/print/forced-colors case.
7. **Render it and look at it.** The validator checks color, not layout - open or
   screenshot the output and eyeball it for label collisions, geometry, and overflow
   before calling it done.

Then check the result against **`references/anti-patterns.md`** - it is the catalog
of what goes wrong. If your chart matches an entry, it's wrong.

## Non-negotiables (true in every design system)

- **Assign categorical hues in fixed order, never cycled.** A 9th series is never a
  generated hue - it folds into "Other," small multiples, or composite encoding.
- **One axis.** Never a dual-axis chart (two y-scales). Two measures of different
  scale -> two charts, small multiples, or indexed to a co

   - Slow path (方案二): `slow_frame_buffer` (1024×4, write-side AGC, hop=128, 87.5% overlap, clears on `gain_changed`), second `xfft_0` via `fft_1024_slow_scheduler` (32 DSP, 513 bins), `slow_harmonic_analyzer` (α-max magnitude, two-pass noise floor, top-8 peaks, phase regression, prior unwrap, robust second pass).
   - Fixed-point identity: N=1024=2^10, Q26 angles, `r20 = r_q26 >>> 6` ⇒ `τ_corr_q10 = Σ w·k·r20 / Σ w·k²`, constant exactly 1.
   - Fusion: `slow_tau_valid` overrides fast vote; requires both pairs pass, `fast_ok`, `mask_learn_done`, ≥1 prior good frame; sanity |τ2 − fast| ≤ 4096 Q10; V bit forced 1 on slow frames; UART T fields remain raw fast-path TDOA.
   - Vote: 23 bins, 128-frame ring, 64-frame early lock, per-frame 23×23 search, 3-point median, confidence `best_score > filled`, fast relock after 12 deviating frames; ~0.20 s.
   - Geometry: measured 2d = 38.6 cm; `COS_K=46588`, `>>>6`; `TAU_MAX=11523`; Σcos² limit 1.25; degenerate (0,0) → θ=90°.
   - Resource budget/measured synth: fast FFT 24 + IFFT 32 + slow FFT 32 + other ≈80 = **168 DSP measured**; baseline 4-parallel was 192 DSP.
   - Measured synth resources: LUT 40961 (64.61%), FF 39576 (31.21%), BRAM 27.5 (20.37%), DSP 168 (70.0%). Slice/WNS/WHS/bit SHA pending implementation.
   - UART 91-byte `$GCCP,...` frame at 115200 8N1; T fields Q10 truncated toward zero; 8-digit 7-seg; SG90 20 ms PWM on C19/C18.
   - Verification ethos: simulation pass ≠ synthesis correctness; check synth log for `8-6104/8-3848/8-988`; every claim needs log/report evidence.
   - Claude

## 一、项目概况

- **器件**：XC7A100T-FGG484-2
- **工具**：Vivado 2018.3
- **设计**：基于 FPGA 的 4 路麦克风 GCC-PHAT 声学定位系统

- **所有模块顶部**：`` `include "project_global_param.vh" ``

1. **添加 include guard**  
   在 `project_global_param.vh` 开头加：
   ```verilog

   `define PROJECT_GLOBAL_PARAM_VH
   ```
   末尾加 `` `endif ``。

   ```
   验证结果：`0`

   ```tcl
   set_property include_dirs {D:/vivado_project/FPGA/GCC-PHAT/GCC-PHAT/GCC-PHAT.srcs/sources_1/new} [current_fileset]
   ```
   验证结果：路径已添加。

   ```tcl
   remove_files [get_files project_global_param.vh]
   ```
   移除后 Sources 窗口不再显示该文件，但错误依旧。

5. **确认所有源文件顶部有 `` `include "project_global_param.vh" ``**  
   已确认。

6. **确认仿真文件集也设置了 include 路径**  
   ```tcl
   set_property include_dirs {D:/.../sources_1/new} [get_filesets sim_1]
   ```

# 📋 基于FPGA的4路麦克风GCC-PHAT无人机声学时延定位系统

## 完整分包任务书（修正版 v1.2）

**适配器件：Xilinx Artix-7 XC7A100T FGG484 | Vivado 2018.3**

---

## 📌 项目核心目标与版本策略

### 当前第一阶段（立即落地）
- **硬件平台**：50MHz主频 Artix-7系列FPGA（目标器件XC7A100T-FGG484）
- **系统参数**：10kHz采样率 × 4路麦克风 × 256点FFT × 24位定点运算
- **核心功能**：4路同步采集 → GCC-PHAT时延估计 → TDOA二维定位 → 串口输出
- **性能指标**：时延估计精度≤1采样点，空间定位误差≤2cm

### 未来升级路径（零代码修改）
- 仅修改全局参数头文件中3个常量（`CUR_CHAN_NUM`、`CUR_SAMPLE_FREQ`、`CUR_FFT_POINT`）
- 直接升级至48kHz × 8路 × 1024点FFT高性能版本
- 90%以上代码完全复用，重新综合生成bit文件即可

### 器件适配说明
- **XC7A100T资源优势**：相比A35T拥有更充裕的逻辑资源（101K LCs, 4860Kb BRAM, 240 DSP48E1）
- **FGG484封装优势**：256个用户IO（fgg484），满足8路I2S麦克风+外设的IO需求
- **Vivado 2018.3兼容性**：FFT IP v9.1需兼容此版本，注意IP配置界面差异

---

## 🔹 项目全局统一约束（所有分包强制执行）

### 1. 时钟与复位规范
- **主时钟**：全系统统一50MHz上升沿触发（使用FGG484的MRCC时钟引脚）
- **复位**：低电平异步复位，内部同步释放（至少打2拍）
- **时钟域**：**严格单一时钟域设计**，禁止任何跨时钟域逻辑
- **时序约束**：所有路径setup/hold在50MHz下零违例
- **FGG484引脚约束**：主时钟建议约束在Bank 14的MRCC引脚（如K17/K18）

### 2. 数据位宽规范
- **采样/FFT位宽**：严格24位补码（`DATA_WIDTH = 24`）
- **中间运算**：乘法结果48位，加法结果25位，按需截断或饱和处理
- **索引/计数**：帧号16位，FFT索引10位，通道索引3位
- **绝对禁止**：任何模块私自裁剪中间运算位宽

### 3. 参数化可复用规范
- **全局参数**：所有模块统一include `project_global_param.vh`
- **禁止硬编码**：通道数、采样率、FFT点数相关数值**严禁写成固定数字**
- **条件编译**：当前版本不需要的逻辑用`generate if`包裹，综合自动裁剪

### 4. 全局参数头文件（唯一参数源）
```verilog
// ============================================
// project_global_param.vh - 全局统一参数定义
// 适配器件：XC7A100T-FGG484
// Vivado版本：2018.3
// ============================================
localparam SYS_CLK_FREQ      = 50_000_000;   // 系统主频50MHz（硬件固定）
localparam DATA_WIDTH        = 24;           // 全局采样/FFT位宽24位
localparam COORD_WIDTH       = 16;           // 空间坐标位宽（单位：cm）
localparam FRAME_CNT_WIDTH   = 16;           // 帧号计数器位宽
localparam FFT_IDX_WIDTH     = 10;           // FFT索引位宽（支持1024点）
localparam CHAN_IDX_WIDTH    = 3;            // 通道索引位宽（支持8路）
localparam TDOA_WIDTH        = 16;           // TDOA时延值位宽

// ======== 当前落地版本参数（第一阶段） ========
localparam CUR_CHAN_NUM      = 4;            // 当前启用4路麦克风
localparam CUR_SAMPLE_FREQ   = 10_000;       // 当前采样率10kHz
localparam CUR_FFT_POINT     = 256;          // 当前FFT点数256
localparam CUR_PAIR_NUM      = (CUR_CHAN_NUM * (CUR_CHAN_NUM - 1)) / 2; // 配对数量=6

// ======== 最大扩展上限（永久固定，预留架构空间） ========
localparam MAX_CHAN_NUM      = 8;            // 最大支持8路
localparam MAX_SAMPLE_FREQ   = 48_000;       // 最大采样率48kHz
localparam MAX_FFT_POINT     = 1024;         // 最大FFT点数1024
localparam MAX_PAIR_NUM      = (MAX_CHAN_NUM * (MAX_CHAN_NUM - 1)) / 2; // 28组配对

// ======== Vivado 2018.3 FFT IP配置参数 ========
localparam FFT_IP_VERSION    = "9.1";        // Vivado 2018.3内置FFT IP版本
localparam FFT_IP_ARCH       = 3;            // Pipelined Streaming I/O架构
localparam FFT_INPUT_WIDTH   = DATA_WIDTH;   // 输入位宽
localparam FFT_OUTPUT_WIDTH  = DATA_WIDTH;   // 输出位宽
localparam FFT_PHASE_FACTOR  = 24;           // 相位因子位宽
```

---

## 🔗 分包间接口时序协议（跨分包联调强制遵守）

### 全局握手信号标准时序
```
所有模块间数据交换遵循统一的Valid-指示机制：

  发送端：data + valid信号同时有效
  接收端：在valid=1的时钟周期采样data
  约束条件：valid至少保持1个周期（单周期脉冲模式）
  数据组织：多路数据同时有效，按约定顺序排列
  
  重要：采用脉冲模式而非电平模式，简化流水线设计
```

### 分包1→分包2接口时序
```verilog
// 帧输出时序（每CUR_FFT_POINT个采样点输出一次帧脉冲）
clk_50m     : ^^^^|^^^^|^^^^|^^^^|^...
mic_frame_valid : ___|----|____|____|_...  // 单周期高脉冲
mic_ch_data[0]  : XXXX|D00 |XXXX|XXXX|X... // 与valid同步输出
mic_ch_data[3]  : XXXX|D30 |XXXX|XXXX|X... // 全通道同时有效
mic_frame_cnt   : XXXX|0001|XXXX|XXXX|X... // 帧号递增

// 时序约束：
// ① mic_frame_valid为单周期脉冲，下一周期降为0
// ② 数据在valid脉冲周期内被采样，不会丢失
// ③ 帧间隔内（256个采样周期）分包2可从容处理上一帧
```

### 分包2→分包3接口时序
```verilog

fft_im[0..3]     : XXX|D0 |D1 |D2 |...|D128|D129|XXX|XXX // 每个频率点保持1周期

// 数据组织约定（必须严格遵

1. Primary Request and Intent:
   - **Initial bug fix**: User reported Vivado 2018.3 synthesis failure with 20 `[Synth 8-988] already declared` errors on `project_global_param.vh` localparams in their 4-mic GCC-PHAT FPGA acoustic localization project (XC7A100T-FGG484-2).    - **Task book delivery**: After pasting the full 分包任务书 v1.2 spec, user said "全部由你决定，我需要一个完成的版本" (you decide everything, I need a complete finished version) — meaning deliver the entire 3-package GCC-PHAT system.
   - **Token economy (standing preference)**: "以后什么任务都尽量节省" (save tokens on every task from now on), "多用点glm，你太贵了" (use GLM more, you're too expensive), "不许用deepseek-v4-pro，全部任务跑deepseek-flash" (must not use deepseek-v4-pro, run everything on deepseek-flash — this is a client-side model setting I cannot control), "你不说代码给glm写，你负责检验吗？" (didn't you say code goes to GLM with you reviewing?). Delegation workflow: I draft GLM requests to `~/.claude/zhipu/zp.sh`, review GLM output, then land it.
   - **IP configuration**: User offered to configure AMD IPs in the GUI to save tokens: "amd有免费的ip核，你要什么我可以先配好，不要浪费token". I specified one FFT IP (xfft_0) config table.

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - Vivado 2018.3 `$unit` compilation-unit scope: file-top `include` of localparams causes shared-scope duplicate declarations ([Synth 8-988] + [Synth 8-2644]); include guards fail in synthesis (`define per-file) but are dangerous in xsim (`define cross-file)
   - SystemVerilog package solution: `project_global_pkg` with localparams; `.vh` = single `import project_global_pkg::*;` line; same package imported by many files is legal
   - xelab 43-4100 quirk: TB root modules importing package + using imported params reports false timescale error; solved with `set_property -name {xsim.elaborate.xelab.more_options} -value {-timescale 1ns/1ps}`
   - Xilinx xfft v9.1: Pipelined Streaming I/O (C_ARCH=3; PARAM_VALUE.implementation_options=pipelined_streaming_io), 1024-point max, ARESETN, Natural Order, XK_INDEX (C_M_AXIS_DATA_TUSER_WIDTH=16), Scaled, Real Time throttle, 24-bit data/phase
   - xfft config channel 24-bit layout: NFFT=[3:0], FWD_INV=[8] (1=FWD), SCALE_SCH=[18:9] (2 bits per radix-2 pair stage: 00=÷1, 01=÷2, 10=÷4, 11=÷8); config takes effect only at frame boundaries (tlast) — so run-time NFFT switching is unreliable for the first frame
   - **Zero-padding frame design**: IP always runs 1024-point frames; each channel = 256 data + 768 zeros per frame (4 frames per batch); zero-padded spectrum → effective bins at k=4b; avoids time-domain concatenation aliasing (bins mod 4 mixing, verified mathematically)
   - IFFT zero-padding: 1024-point conjugate-symmetric input (0..128 direct, 129..895 zeros, 896..1023 conjugate) = 4× time-domain interpolation; tdoa_q10 = (peak_idx<512 ? peak_idx : peak_idx-1024) × 256 + δ/4
   - 2D BRAM arrays with variable first-dimension index fail RAM inference → registers (caused 170K FF overflow); fix: per-channel generate with 1-D arrays + parallel read + mux
   - CORDIC two-stage pipeline (20 vectoring + 20 rotation stages), z in Q26 per 2π, atan table (atan(2^-i)/2π×2^26), X0=2^22/K=2547003, W=28-bit signed, small-value guard at |x20|<2635
   - Hyperbolic closed-form TDOA solver: X=400(e1+m2)m1e2/(e1e2−m1m2), Q10 fixed-point, serial restoring division (div_serial) and serial sqrt (sqrt_serial)
   - Parabolic peak interpolation: delta = 0.5(L−R)/(L−2P+R), serial division
   - GLM delegation via curl: `~/.claude/zhipu/zp.sh` reads key from `~/.claude/zhipu/key.txt` (chmod 600), model glm-4-flash
   - Token-economy mandate: concise replies, r

   - `(expression)[range]` 部分选择在 Verilog 中是非法的——需要中间 wire

   - 对完整 48 位打包 `{im[23:0], re[23:0]}` 字段进行 `$atan2` 获得 PHAT 相位；用于验证的 `$signed` 转换
   - xsim 分层引用从 TB 探针访问 DUT 内部信号（`uut.u_scheduler.phat_buf[k]`）

2. **Key Technical Concepts:**
   - CORDIC vectoring-mode convergence: canonical rule is `if (y < 0) → x'=x−y·d, y'=y+x·d, z'=z−atan`, `else → x'=x+y·d, y'=y−x·d, z'=z+atan`. Inverting the condition makes the vector diverge and z saturate at ∓99.9° (= ±Σatan table)
   - CORDIC with no valid signal free-runs: a zero input produces the idle 99.9° saturation value
   - Quadrant pre-rotation (negate x,y when re_in<0) + output flip (flip_pipe tap) to extend the CORDIC range to ±180°; the flip bit must be tapped at the exact pipeline-aligned index
   - Pipeline latency counting: input at cycle T → xv[0] at T+1 → xv[20] at T+21 → zr[0] at T+22 → xr[20] at T+42 → re_out registered at T+43; `flip_pipe[k] at cycle C = re_in[23] at cycle C−(k+1)`, so **tap index 41**
   - Vivado xsim 2018.3 toolchain: `/d/vivado/Vivado/2018.3/bin/{xvlog,xelab,xsim,xvhdl}.bat`; xvlog needs `-sv -i <srcdir>`; xelab needs `-sv -timescale 1ns/1ps -L secureip -L unisims_ver`; `rm -rf xsim.dir` must run BEFORE xvlog; `project_global_pkg.sv` must be compiled before files that `include project_global_param.vh`
   - xfft v9.1 24-bit config word layout (authoritative from the IP's own demo TB): `tdata[3:0]=NFFT`, `tdata[8]=FWD/INV`, `tdata[18:9]=SCALE_SCH`, rest reserved
   - Default scaling schedule for the IP: first stage pair "11" (>>3), subsequent pairs "10" (>>2), unused high pairs "00"
   - IFFT direction semantics for GCC-PHAT: `IFFT(A·conj(B))` peaks at `−Δ` where Δ = delay of a relative to b ⇒ TDOA(A,B) = τ_b − τ_a; a forward FFT instead gives +Δ (opposite sign)
   - Parabolic peak interpolation: Δ = 0.5(L−R)/(L−2P+R); at a true peak the denominator L+R−2P is always negative, so sign(Δ) = sign(L−R)⊕sign(den); |L−R| ≤ |den| always holds at a peak
   - Restoring (not non-restoring) shift-subtract division; the fractional form requires dividend < divisor and yields Q16 for 16 iterations
   - 1024-point IFFT with a 256-bin PHAT spectrum placed at positions 0..128 + conjugate mirror at 896..1023 = 4× sinc interpolation; peak index = 4×τ
   - 4-mic cross array geometry: mic 0=(−20,0), 1=(+20,0), 2=(0,−20), 3=(0,+20) cm; 34300 cm/s, 10 kHz → 3.43 cm/sample

2. **Key Technical Concepts:**
   - **TDOA sign convention (now confirmed end-to-end)**: with the FFT config word fixed, `IFFT(A·conj(B))` for pair (a,b) with i<j yields reported TDOA(a,b) = τ_a − τ_b (in Q10 samples), which exactly matches `tdoa_solver`'s requirement (τ01 = τ₀−τ₁, τ23 = τ₂−τ₃; x = +√X·sign(τ01), y = +√Y·sign(τ23)). No sign flip needed anywhere.
   - **xfft v9.1 config word layout (authoritative, verified in sim)**: `[3:0]=NFFT`, `[8]=FWD_INV`, `[18:9]=SCALE_SCH`, rest reserved. Default 1024-point schedule gives total 1/2048 scale (verified: DC frame 2²² → 2048).
   - **Hyperbolic closed-form solve** (verified algebraically against the code): with m = (Δr/2d)², e = 1−m, d = 20 cm: x² = 400·(e₁+m₂)·m₁·e₂/(e₁e₂−m₁m₂); y² = 400·(m₁+e₂)·m₂·e₁/(e₁e₂−m₁m₂). |Δr| ≥ 2d ⇒ invalid.
   - **Fixed-point Q-format arithmetic**: dr (Q10 cm) → ×3.43; m must be computed as `((dr*dr) >>> 20) * 41 >>> 6` (Q20→real, then ×1024/1600 = ×41/64); de in Q20 (max 2²⁰, needs 21+ bits); quotient nx(Q30)/de(Q20) = X (Q10).
   - **Restoring integer divider**: correct form iterates over NUM_W numerator bits with `rem` initialized to 0 and the numerator shifted in from the MSB; the buggy "Q-fractional" form (`{num, {DIV_CYC{1'b0}}}`) computes ⌊num·2^(2n)/den⌋ which overflows.
   - **Registered-read BRAM semantics**: `rd_data` is valid one cycle after `rd_en`/`rd_addr`.
   - **Ping-pong frame buffer throughput**: reader needs 4ch×256×3 = 3072 cycles/frame, so the write rate must be slower than that (real system: 10 kHz/256-pt = 1.28M cycles/frame).
   - Vivado xsim 2018.3 harness: `sim.sh <workdir> <top> <srcdir> <files...>` with `VHDL=...` env var; xvlog `-sv -i <dir>`; xelab `-sv -timescale 1ns/1ps -L secureip -L unisims_ver`; `rm -rf xsim.dir` before xvlog; `project_global_pkg.sv` compiled first.
   - SystemVerilog gotcha: **`solve` is a reserved keyword** (solve…before constraint syntax).

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - **Standard I2S protocol (one-bit delay)**: after LRCLK edge, the RX skips the first BCLK rising edge and samples 24 bits on the next 24 rising edges; the TX must place the MSB on the line at the **first falling edge after LRCLK**. TBs that inserted an extra filler bit produced received data = expected >> 1.
   - **clk_gen**: LRCLK toggles on `bclk_fall` at `lrck_half_cnt==31`; 64 BCLK per LRCLK period; BCLK=640 kHz, LRCLK=10 kHz, sample_pulse, tick_1ms (TICK_DIV = 50000).
   - **Registered-read BRAM semantics**: `rd_data` valid one cycle after `rd_en`/`rd_addr`.
   - **Frame-buffer throughput**: reader needs 4ch×256×3 = 3072 cycles/frame, so the testbench write rate must be slower (settled on 20 cycles/sample = 5120 cycles/frame, 66% margin).
   - **Vivado 7-series I/O rules**: `K20`, `M14`, `L17` are VCCO power pins and `E20` is GND on xc7a100tfgg484 (verified via `get_package_pins` after `link_design`); a clock input driving a BUFG must be on an MRCC/SRCC pin (`J19 = IO_L12P_T1_MRCC_15`), not a DQS pin (`K17 = IO_L21P_T3_DQS_15` → Place 30-574 error); every port needs an explicit IOSTANDARD or it defaults to LVCMOS18 and conflicts with 3.3 V neighbours in the same bank (DRC BIVC-1).
   - **XDC Tcl syntax**: `[get_ports {mic_pdm_sdin[*]}]` (braces) is required for vector/range port matching; unbraced `[get_ports mic_pdm_sdin[7:0]]` matched nothing after synthesis trimmed unused bits.
   - **xfft v9.1 config**: 1024-point, pipelined_streaming_io, runtime-configurable length, 24-bit fixed point, natural order, real-time throttle, scaled, round_half_up; default scale schedule = 1/2048.
   - **tb_fft_chain requires `hanning_256.mem` in the simulation working directory** (the `$readmemh` path is relative) — otherwise ROM contents are X and all FFT outputs are X.

## 1) sources_1/new/uart_tx_module.sv

### 91 字节帧布局(0 起偏移)

帧格式注释(L2-7):
```
// 帧格式（任务书规范，91 字节）：
//   $GCCP,NNNN,T01:+SSS,T02:+SSS,T03:+SSS,T12:+SSS,T13:+SSS,T23:+SSS,
//   X:+DDDDD,Y:-DDDDD,V:1*HH\r\n
//   校验和 HH = $ 之后至 * 之前全部字符的 XOR（2 位大写十六进制）
```

布局注释(L68-79):
```
// 布局（0 起）：
//   0..5  "$GCCP,"
//   6..9  帧号 4 位
//   10    ','
//   每配对（pair 0..5）：'T' + 2 位配对号 + ':' + 符号 + 3 位值 + ','
//     pair p: base = 11 + p*9 → T(0) 号(1,2) :(3) 符(4) 值(5,6,7) ,(8)
//   X 字段：base = 11+54=65 → 'X'(0) ':'(1) 符(2) 值(3..7) ','(8)
//   Y 字段：base = 74 → 同 X
//   83..86 "V:1*"
//   87,88  校验和 2 位
//   89 '\r' 90 '\n'
```

逐字节展开:

| 偏移 | 内容 | 偏移 | 内容 |
|---|---|---|---|
| 0..5 | `$GCCP,` | 47..55 | `T13:+SSS,` |
| 6..9 | 帧号 NNNN(4 位十进制补零) | 56 | `T`(pair5=23) |
| 10 | `,` | 57 | `2`、58 `3` |
| 11 | `T`(pair0=01) | 59 | `:`、60 符号、61..63 值、64 `,` |
| 12 | `0`、13 `1` | 65 | `X` |
| 14 | `:`、15 符号、16..18 值、19 `,` | 66 | `:` |
| 20 | `T`(pair1=02) | 67 | X 符号(+/-) |
| 21 | `0`、22 `2` | 68..72 | X 值 5 位十进制补零 |
| 23 | `:`、24 符号、25..27 值、28 `,` | 73 | `,` |
| 29 | `T`(pair2=03) | 74 | `Y` |
| 30 | `0`、31 `3` | 75 | `:` |
| 32 | `:`、33 符号、34..36 值、37 `,` | 76 | Y 符号(+/-) |
| 38 | `T`(pair3=12) | 77..81 | Y 值 5 位十进制补零 |
| 39 | `1`、40 `2` | 82 | `,` |
| 41 | `:`、42 符号、43..45 值、46 `,` | 83..86 | `V:1*` |
| | | 87,88 | 校验和 2 位大写十六进制 |
| | | 89 | `\r`(0x0D)、90 `\n`(0x0A) |

字段选择函数 f_sel(L80-102)与 static_char 查表(L104-127)、配对号字符 pair_char(L131-145)共同实现。配对号为 T01,T02,T03,T12,T13,T23(L130 注释)。

### X/Y 坐标整数→ASCII 的生成方式

符号字符(L191-196,X 字段;Y 同理 L202-207):
```systemverilog
4: begin  // X 符号
    out_char &lt;= (px &lt; 0) ? "-" : "+";
    char_ready &lt;= 1'b1;
    cks &lt;= cks ^ ((px &lt; 0) ? "-" : "+");
    char_idx &lt;= char_idx + 1'b1;
end
```

数值装载(L197-201,负号处理就是取绝对值):
```systemverilog
5: begin  // X 5 位
    work_val &lt;= (px &lt; 0) ? -px : px;
    pow10    &lt;= 10000;
    gstate   &lt;= G_DIGIT;
end
```

逐位慢速减除法(G_DIGIT 状态,L224-240),每位先连续减 pow10 计数、减不动时输出 `'0'+cur_digit`,权值从 10000 递减到 1,共 5 位,**全部补零输出**(含前导 0),即 BCD 式逐位提取而非 printf 式去前导零:
```systemverilog
G_DIGIT: begin
    if (work_val &gt;= pow10) begin
        work_val  &lt;= work_val - pow10;
        cur_digit &lt;= cur_digit + 1'b1;
    end else begin
        out_char   &lt;= 8'h30 + {4'b0, cur_digit};
        char_ready &lt;= 1'b1;
        cks        &lt;= cks ^ (8'h30 + {4'b0, cur_digit});
        cur_digit  &lt;= 0;
        // 结束当前字段
        if (pow10 == 1) begin
            char_idx &lt;= char_idx + 1'b1;
            gstate   &lt;= G_NEXT;
        end else
            pow10 &lt;= pow10 / 10;
    end
end
```
负数处理:仅符号位输出 `'-'`,数值部分输出绝对值(`-px`)。px 为 16 位有符号数,值域 ±32767,5 位字段实际足够(若超过 5 位,cur_digit 会超过 9 产生非十进制字符,无饱和保护)。帧号(4 位,L171-176)和 TDOA(3 位,L183-190,TDOA 为 Q10,先 `&gt;&gt;&gt;10` 折算成采样点整数再取绝对值)用同一 G_DIGIT 机制。

### 模块端口(L16-26)

module uart_tx_module (
    input  wire         clk_50m,
    input  wire         rst_n,
    input  wire         frame_valid,           // 每帧结果就绪脉冲
    input  wire [FRAME_CNT_WIDTH-1:0] frame_cnt,

    input  wire [COORD_WIDTH-1:0] pos_y_cm,
    input  wire         pos_valid,
    output wire         uart_tx
);
```
参数来自 project_global_pkg.sv:`COORD_WIDTH = 16`(cm,补码有符号整数,L16)、`TDOA_WIDTH = 16`(Q10 采样点,L20)、`MAX

# 无人机测向输出级改造方案

## A. 各文件改动方案

### A1. tdoa_solver.sv（重写数学，端口不变输入侧）

**端口变化**：输入 `tdoa_result/all_done/mic_coord_x/y/pre_frame_cnt/mask_learn_done` 全保留（mic_coord/pre_frame_cnt 同现状不用）；输出改为：
```systemverilog
output reg signed [15:0] target_azimuth_01deg, target_elevation_01deg;  // 0.1°
output reg               direction_valid;
```

**定点格式与常数（全部经精确推导）**：
- cos 用 **Q23 有符号**（不用 Q16/Q24）：cos=1 映射 2^23 后，最高位恒 0，可直接 `&lt;&lt;1` 拼成 CORDIC 的 Q24 有符号输入（Q24 的 cos=1 会让 bit23=1 被 CORDIC 误判为负触发 flip——Q23 是输入域对齐的关键）。
- 乘法常数：cos = τ_q10·c/(2d·fs·1024) = τ×8.3740234375e-5；×2^23 = **702.464** → 取 `(τ×1405)&gt;&gt;&gt;1`（=τ×702.5，误差 51ppm，等价 0.003° 量级）：
  ```systemverilog
  wire signed [26:0] ca_t = $signed(tau01) * 27'sd1405;
  ca &lt;= ca_t &gt;&gt;&gt; 1;   // Q23 有符号 23 位
  ```
- 平方：`ca2 &lt;= ca*ca`（23×23=46 位 Q46，两补码自平方恒正）；`sum47 &lt;= {1'b0,ca2}+{1'b0,cb2}`（47 位，bit46=1 判超界）。
- cosγ：`w &lt;= 47'h400000000000 - sum47;`（2^46−Σcos²，非退化时 Σ≥1 无借位），`rad &lt;= w[45:0]`。
- sqrt 复用：`sqrt_serial #(.IN_W(46),.OUT_W(23),.SQRT_CYC(23))`——rad_hi2/rem_shift/sub_val 位宽公式核对无误，rad&lt;2^46 恒成立（退化旁路+有效性门控），root 23 位无溢出。
- CORDIC 复用：`cordic_phat_norm_24b u_cord(...)`，re_out/im_out 悬空；输入 `cord_re &lt;= {ca,1'b0}`（Q23&lt;&lt;1=Q24）。
- 0.1° 换算：0.1° = 2^26/3600 → `deg01 = (|angle|×225 + 2^21)&gt;&gt;&gt;22`（3600/2^26 = 225/2^22 精确），φ 补符号；θ 恒正直接算。
- 有效域：|τ|&gt;11941（=2^23/(1405/2)）或 Σcos²&gt;1（sum47[46]）或学习期 → invalid；τ01==τ23==0 → 退化。

**状态机**（`S_IDLE/COS/CHK/SUM/SQRT1/SQRT2/CORD/DEG/OUT`，每状态动作见 B 节）。div_serial 不再实例化（新数学无除法），删除 u_div 相关代码。

### A2. cordic_phat_norm_24b.sv（只加不改）
新增输出端口（详见 C 节），现有 re_out/im_out/flip_pipe/vec_small 逻辑一行不动。

### A3. uart_tx_module.sv
- 端口改名 `pos_x_cm/pos_y_cm → pos_azimuth_01deg/pos_elevation_01deg`，内部 px/py→pa/pe（16 位 0.1° 有符号）。
- f_sel：X 区(65..73)→A 区：`i==67→f_sel=4`（符号不变）；`i==68..70→f_sel=5`；`i==72→f_sel=9`（小数位，新增）；71 落入 else（static）。Y 区(74..82)→E 区：`i==76→6`；`77..79→7`；`i==81→f_sel=10`；80→static。
- static_char：65:`"A"`（原"X"）、74:`"E"`（原"Y"）、71/80:`"."` 新增。
- 字段装载（原 case 5/7 改 3 位整数+1 位小数）：
  ```systemverilog
  5: begin   // A 区 3 位整数
      work_val &lt;= (pa&lt;0)? (-pa)/10 : pa/10;   // int3
      frac_dig &lt;= (pa&lt;0)? ((-pa)%10) : (pa%10); // 小数位锁存
      pow10 &lt;= 100; gstate &lt;= G_DIGIT;
  end
  9: begin   // A 区小数位（char_idx=72）
      out_char &lt;= 8'h30 + {4'b0,frac_dig}; char_ready&lt;=1;
      cks &lt;= cks ^ (8'h30+{4'b0,frac_dig}); char_idx&lt;=char_idx+1;
  end
  // 7/10 同构（E 区）
  ```
  新增 `reg [3:0] frac_dig;`。G_DIGIT 主体（减除法）零改动，pow10 终值 100 自动收尾到 char_idx 71 → static '.' → 72 → f_sel 9。
- **校验和修复**：
  - case 8（偏移 87/88）：`out_char &lt;= (char_idx==8'd87) ? hex_char(cks[7:4]) : hex_char(cks[3:0]);`（原来两拍都发高半字节）。
  - f_sel=0 分支的 XOR 加区间条件（'$' 与 '*' 不计入）：`cks &lt;= (char_idx&gt;=8'd1 &amp;&amp; char_idx&lt;=8'd85) ? (cks^ch) : cks;`（覆盖 '$'@0、'*'@86、校验和@87/88、CR/LF@89/90 的排除；动态字段全在 [1,85] 内无需条件）。

### A4. phat_position_top.sv / top_gcc_phat.sv
纯改名：`target_x_cm→target_azimuth_01deg`、`target_y_cm→target_elevation_01deg`、`position_valid→direction_valid`；内部 wire `x_cm/y_cm→az01/el01`、`pos_valid_raw/gated→dir_valid_raw/gated`；uart 例化 `.pos_azimuth_01deg(az01),.pos_elevation_01deg(el01)`。top 内 wire `target_x/target_y→target_azimuth_01deg/target_elevation_01deg`、`position_valid→direction_valid`。board_led 已核实由 mic_collect_top 驱动、与 position_valid 无关，**不改**。

2. 关键技术概念：
   - **DOA / 远场方向余弦测向：** τ01 = 2d·cosα·fs/c，τ23 = 2d·cosβ·fs/c；cosα = τ01·c/(2d·fs) = τ01 × 8.3740234375e-5；cosγ = √(1−cos²α−cos²β)（z>0 分支）；φ = atan2(cosβ, cosα) ∈ (−180°,+180°]，0°=+x，正值朝 +y；θ = atan2(cosγ, √(cos²α+cos²β)) ∈ [0°,+90°]。4 麦克风共面阵列具有精确的 z↔−z 镜像模糊（TDOA 仅依赖于 z²）。
   - **定点选择：** cos 使用 **Q23 signed**（cos=1→2^23 保持 bit23 为 0，因此 `{ca,1'b0}` → Q24 不会触发 CORDIC 的翻转预处理）；ca = (τ × 1405) >>> 1（≈τ×702.5，51 ppm ≈ 0.003°）；平方 → Q46；Σcos² > 2^46 ⇒ 无效；0.1° 换算 deg01 = (|angle|×225 + 2^21) >>> 22（3600/2^26 = 225/2^22 精确）。
   - **CORDIC 角度引出：** 内部 zv[20] 为 Q26，其中 2^26 = 2π（26 位二进制补码中 ±2^25 = ±180°）。在输入周期 T：xv[0]/zv[0] 在 T+1 → zv[20] 在 T+21；flip_pipe[20] 在 T+21 对齐；angle_out（寄存）在 **T+22** 有效。校正：`flip_pipe[20] ? zv[20] + 26'sd33554432 : zv[20]`。
   - **近场效应：** 2D 平面内声源 (10,10,0) cm 产生 cosα = (√1000−√200)/40 = 0.43704，而不是 0.7071，因此 DOA 公式报告 θ ≈ 51.8° 而非 0°——这在远场假设下在数学上是正确的。
   - **Vivado 流程：** `-mode batch -source <tcl>`；run_impl.tcl 中使用 `create_project -in_memory`（无 .xpr 锁）；.xpr 中的 `used_in_synthesis/implementation=0` 表现为 `<File>` 条目**没有 `<FileInfo>` 块**。
   - **基线实现结果（DOA 变更前）：** WNS +0.584 ns，WHS +0.039 ns，TNS=THS=0，341 个 DRC 违规 / 0 个 Error。

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - **Far-field DOA / direction cosines**: τ01 = 2d·cosα·fs/c, τ23 = 2d·cosβ·fs/c; cosα = τ01×8.3740234375e-5 (=702.464/2^23) with τ in **Q10 samples**; cosγ = √(1−cos²α−cos²β) (z>0 branch); φ = atan2(cosβ,cosα) ∈ (−180°,+180°], 0°=+x, positive toward +y; θ = atan2(cosγ, √(cos²α+cos²β)) ∈ [0°,+90°]. Degenerate τ01=τ23=0 → φ=0°, θ=+90°, valid. Invalid: |τ|>11941 (cos>1) or Σcos²>2^46.
   - **Fixed point (corrected this session)**: ca/cb are **24-bit signed Q23** (cos=1 ↔ 2^23); ca = (τ×1405)>>>1 ≈ τ×702.5 (51 ppm error, affects only θ, <0.005°). Squares are Q46 in **48-bit** registers; Σ ≤ 2^47; invalid iff Σ > 2^46 (strict). √(Q46) → Q23, so `sqrt_serial #(.IN_W(48), .OUT_W(24), .SQRT_CYC(24))` — **IN_W must equal 2×SQRT_CYC** (2 radicand bits consumed per cycle) or the result is off by √2.
   - **CORDIC (cordic_phat_norm_24b)**: 24-bit input in Q2.22 (unit = 2^22, as driven by tb_cordic_only); 28-bit internal x/y (24+4 guard); z is 26-bit Q26 where 2^26 = 2π (±2^25 = ±180°); latency: input at cycle T → zv[20] at T+21 → angle_out (registered) at **T+22**; `angle_corr = flip_pipe[20] ? zv[20]+2^25 : zv[20]`; `vec_small` (|xv[20]| < 2635 ⟺ input magnitude < 1600) only zeroes re_out/im_out and angle_valid — **angle_out is unaffected**. Feeding Q23 values to the CORDIC requires an arithmetic >>1 (`{ca[23], ca[23:1]}`).
   - **deg01 conversion**: deg01 = (|angle|×225 + 2^21) >>> 22 (3600/2^26 = 225/2^22 exact); −2^25 (==26'h2000000) special-cased to +1800.
   - **tb_tdoa_solver FSM sampling**: at cnt=23 the φ angle is valid, at cnt=24 the θ angle (consecutive 1-cycle-apart CORDIC launches; the free-running pipeline processes both).
   - **Near-field effect**: planar wavefront assumption fails when R ≈ d; e.g. source (10,10,0) cm → cosα=0.4370 instead of 0.7071 → reported θ=51.8° though the geometric sight-line elevation is 0°.
   - **Mirror ambiguity**: coplanar array TDOA depends only on z², so z↔−z are indistinguishable — hence direction (not coordinates) is the natural output.
   - **Vivado xsim flow**: compile `project_global_pkg.sv` first; `xvlog -sv -i <incdir>`; `xelab <top> glbl -timescale 1ns/1ps -L unisims_ver -L secureip` (single-dash `-timescale` on xelab fixes 43-4100; **do not** pass `--debug typical` for long runs — it is ~5× slower); Windows-style `D:/…` paths in file lists (MSYS `/d/…` fails); glbl.v at `D:/vivado/Vivado/2018.3/data/verilog/src/glbl.v`; tools at `/d/vivado/Vivado/2018.3/bin`.
   - **UART frame**: 91 bytes, `$

# Workflow authoring reference

A workflow structures work across many agents — to be comprehensive (decompose and cover in parallel), to be confident (independent perspectives and adversarial checks before committing), or to take on scale one context can't hold (migrations, audits, broad sweeps). The script is where you encode that structure: what fans out, what verifies, what synthesizes.

When you do call it, the right move is often **hybrid**: scout inline first (list the files, find the channels, scope the diff) to discover the work-list, then call Workflow to pipeline over it. You don't need to know the shape before the *task* — only before the *orchestration step*.

Common single-phase workflows you can chain across turns:
- **Understand** — parallel readers over relevant subsystems → structured map
- **Design** — judge panel of N independent approaches → scored synthesis
- **Review** — dimensions → find → adversarially verify (the review-changes example)
- **Research** — multi-modal sweep → deep-read → synthesize
- **Migrate** — discover sites → transform each (worktree isolation) → verify

For larger work, run several in sequence — read each result before deciding the next phase. You stay in the loop; each workflow is one well-scoped fan-out.

**Ultracode.** When a system-reminder confirms ultracode is on, that opt-in is standing: author and run a workflow for every substantive task by default. The goal is the most exhaustive, correct answer you can produce — token cost is not a constraint. For multi-phase work (understand → design → implement → review), that often means several workflows in sequence — one per phase — so you stay in the loop between them. The quality patterns below (adversarial verify, multi-modal sweep, completeness critic, loop-until-dry) are the tools; pick what fits the task. Lean toward orchestrating with workflows and adversarially verifying your findings — unless the work is trivial or already verified. Solo only on conversational turns or trivial mechanical edits. When a reminder says ultracode is off, revert to the opt-in rule in the Workflow tool description.

Pass the script inline via `script` — do not Write it to a file first. Every invocation automatically persists its script to a file under the session directory and returns the path in the tool result. To iterate on a workflow, edit that file with Write/Edit and re-invoke Workflow with `{scriptPath: "<path>"}` instead of resending the full script.

Every script must begin with `export const meta = {...}`:
  export const meta = {
    name: 'find-flaky-tests',
    description: 'Find flaky tests and propose fixes',   // one-line, shown in permission dialog
    phases: [                                            // one entry per phase() call
      { title: 'Scan', detail: 'grep test logs for retries' },
      { title: 'Fix', detail: 'one agent per flaky test' },
    ],
  }
  // script body starts here — use agent()/parallel()/pipeline()/phase()/log()
  phase('Scan')
  const flaky = await agent('grep CI logs for retry markers', {schema: FLAKY_SCHEMA})
  ...

The `meta` object must be a PURE LITERAL — no variables, function calls, spreads, or template interpolation. Required fields: `name`, `description`. Optional: `whenToUse` (shown in the workflow list), `phases`. Use the SAME phase titles in meta.phases as in phase() calls — titles are matched exactly; a phase() call with no matching meta entry just gets its own progress group. Add `model` to a phase entry when that phase uses a specific model override.

Script body hooks:
- agent(prompt: string, opts?: {label?: string, phase?: string, schema?: object, model?: string, effort?: string, isolation?: 'worktree', agentType?: string}): Promise<any> — spawn a subagent. Without schema, returns its final text as a string. With schema (a JSON Schema), the subagent is forced to call a StructuredOutput tool and agent() returns the validated object — no parsing needed. Returns null if the user skips t

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - **8-mic/48 kHz parameterization**: `CUR_CHAN_NUM=4→8`, `CUR_SAMPLE_FREQ=10_000→48_000`, `TDOA_WIDTH=16→17`; `CUR_PAIR_NUM` auto 6→28; acquisition/preprocessing/pair-table all parameterized (zero code change).
   - **clk_gen accumulator**: compares `bclk_acc_next >= SYS_CLK` (not 2^N), `BCLK_FREQ=64×fs`, `INC=2×BCLK_FREQ=6,144,000` at 48 kHz, `BCLK_ACC_WIDTH=$clog2(SYS_CLK+INC+1)=26`; BCLK=3.072 MHz, average 0 ppm, half-period toggles 8/9 cycles (160/180 ns vs ideal 162.76 ns); LRCK/sample_pulse = BCLK/64 by counting 31/63 toggles.
   - **Solver math at 48 kHz**: `cos_Q23 = τ_Q10 × 146.3467`; recommended `COS_K=32'sd4683` with `>>>5` (=146.34375, −19.93 ppm) vs current `1405>>>1` (702.5); `TAU_MAX=57,320` (physical 2d·fs/c = 55.9767 samples = 57,320.12 Q10); `TAU_MAX` must be **17-bit** (`localparam signed [16:0] TAU_MAX = 17'sd57320;`) because `16'sd57320` truncates to −8,216; ca max at τ=57320 is 8,388,423 < 2^23−1=8,388,607; no-overflow limit τ=57,321.
   - **TDOA width overflow**: physical |τ|max at 48 kHz = 57,320 (Q10) > 16-bit signed 32,767 → current hardcoded clamp in `ifft_peak_detect.sv:202-204` (±32767/−32768) saturates all directions with |cosα|>0.5716 (cone ~55.14° around the endfire axis, ≈43% of solid angle); requires TDOA_WIDTH=17 plus widened clamp/w sum.
   - **Pair index trap**: `pair_scheduler` enumerates i<j; at 8 channels (2,3)=13 not 5; generic formula `CUR_TAU_Y_IDX = 2*CUR_CHAN_NUM-3` (4 ch→5, 8 ch→13); `tdoa_solver.sv` hardcodes `tdoa_result[0]`/`[5]` in 8 references across lines 155–162 (tau01/tau23/cos_bad/deg_case).
   - **UCA geometry (corrected)**: 8 mics at 45° spacing — (0,2)&(1,3) are NOT diameters (28.28 cm, 45° apart); true perpendicular diameters are (0,4)&(2,6) = indices 3 & 16, or (1,5)&(3,7) = indices 10 & 21.
   - **UART bandwidth**: frame period 266,667 cycles vs 91-byte frame 394,918 cycles at BAUD_DIV=434 → conflict; BAUD_DIV=217 (230400) → 197,470 ✓; 28-pair frame = 11+9×N+26 = 289 bytes requires 921600 (BAUD_DIV=54, 156,060); `UART_PAIR=6` must not be changed (byte positions hardcoded in `knd` decode).
   - **Per-pair timing**: ~1222 cycles (129 FREQ_LOOP + 48 FREQ_TAIL + 1024 IFFT_SEND + ~21 PEAK_WAIT); 28 pairs ≈ 34.2k; total frame processing ≈ 49k of 266,667 cycles.

Mask energy uses **ch0 only**. Bin spacing = fs/256 = **39.0625 Hz** at 10 kHz; verified by tb_fft_chain (1 kHz → bin 26).
   - **Mask threshold truth (my key finding)**: `energy_x3 = {energy,2'b00} + {2'b00,energy}` = **5E** (comment wrongly says 3E); `accum_x2[26:1]` = accum = 3×mean → pass ⇔ 5E > 3·mean ⇔ **E > 0.6×mean** (comment wrongly says ×2). Numerically verified (mean=100 → E=61 passes, E=60 fails).
   - **Recommended rewrite** (doc/07 §4): `wire [26:0] e3 = {energy, 1'b0} + energy; wire pass = (e3 > {accum[idx], 1'b0});` (⇒ E > 2×mean); variants: 1× = `e3 > accum[idx]`, 4× = `e3 > {accum[idx],2'b00}`.
   - **Band gate (doc/07 §3)**: package localparams `MASK_BIN_LO`/`MASK_BIN_HI`, and `freq_valid_mask[idx] <= pass && (idx >= MASK_BIN_LO) && (idx <= MASK_BIN_HI);`. Band with no passing bins → all-zero mask → V:0 (doubles as "no target in band" indicator).

   - **Mask semantics**: computed from **channel 0's energy only** (`fft_re[0]`), yet `pair_scheduler.sv:270` pipelines that single mask (`wr_msk <= {wr_msk[PIPE_DLY-2:0], freq_valid_mask[freq_cnt]}`) and applies it to **all 6 pairs** at `pair_scheduler.sv:279`: `phat_buf[addr_pipe[PIPE_DLY-1]] <= (wr_msk[PIPE_DLY-1] && !learn_mask_zero) ? <cordic_out> : 0;` — a proper inclusion mask.
   - **Bin mapping**: 129 bins of a 256-point real FFT; bin spacing 10 kHz/256 = 39.0625 Hz; k=26 → 1016 Hz; k=127 → 4961 Hz.
   - **Delay/τ mapping**: 1024-point IFFT index → τ_Q10 = idx_centered × 256.
   - **Truth values**: φ=45°, θ=30° → τ01 = τ23 = **+7313 Q10** (7.141 samples). Interferer at φ=180°: τ01 = **−11942**, τ23 = **0** (it lies on the y-axis, so its differential delay for pair (2,3) is exactly zero — the worst case).
   - **PHAT whitening theory**: after whitening, every bin has unit weight, so a weak-but-coherent bin is worth as much as a strong one; removing bins by energy is nearly orthogonal to what PHAT needs. A narrowband interferer can only dominate if it occupies a large fraction of bins (random-walk argument: need C_coherent ≳ 3-4×sqrt(M_incoherent)).
   - **Frequency-domain stimulus injection**: per frame, 129 beats of `pre_frame_valid` with `fft_idx` 0..128, each carrying 4 channels of 24-bit signed re/im; delay applied as phase `e^{−j2πk·Δt/256}`; global scale so max|X| = 2^22.
   - **xsim quirks**: Git Bash→cmd splits `-testplusarg MEM=...` at `=` (must use an option file with `-f`); mem paths must be Windows-style (`D:/...`), NOT MS

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - **The root-cause bug**: `cordic_phat_norm_24b.sv`'s `vec_small` gate was 21 cycles early relative to the data it gates. `xv[20]` is valid at T+21 for input T (segment 1: `xv[0]` at T+1 + 20 stages); `xr[20]`→`re_fin` is valid at T+42 (segment 2: `xr[0]` at T+22 + 20 stages) and is sampled by the output register at T+42. So bin k's output was gated by bin (k+21)'s magnitude.
   - **CORDIC latency bookkeeping**: input presented during cycle X → `xv[0]`@X+1 → `xv[20]`@X+21 → `zr[0]`/`xr[0]`@X+22 → `xr[20]`@X+42 → `re_out`@X+43 = 43 cycles. `angle_out` is valid at X+22 and `angle_valid <= ~vec_small` at the same edge is CORRECT (uses the T+21 `vec_small` = input X) — do not change it.
   - **`pair_scheduler.sv:56`**: `localparam integer PIPE_DLY = 48;` = 读(1)+mux(1)+mult(3)+cordic(43) — the scheduler's write path alignment is correct.
   - **The gate threshold**: `xv[20] = K·|v|` with K = Π√(1+2^−2i) ≈ 1.64676 (input is a sign-extended 24-bit integer, not Q24). The original `28'sd2635` therefore meant |v| < 1600, whereas the code comment's intent ("R<100 LSB (Q24)") implies |v| < 100 → threshold 100×K ≈ 165.
   - **Evidence method**: model "bin k kept ⟺ mask[k] ∧ |v[k+j]| ≥ T" and scan (j, T) against the DUT's recorded τ values; the true RTL matched uniquely at j=21, T=1600~1700.
   - **Stimulus semantics**: the scheduler feeds one bin per clock to the CORDIC, so a cycle offset = a bin offset. `x16.mem` = with a strong low-band interferer; `y16.mem` = clean; both 27 frames (3 learn + 24 measure) × 129 bins × 4 ch × 2 (re/im) = 27864 lines.
   - **Mask semantics**: `freq_valid_mask` is an input PORT of `phat_position_top`; the TB's `mask_applied` (latched at `end_pulse`) drives it; the TB replica was proven bit-identical to a parallel `freq_noise_mask` instance. The effective threshold is E > 0.6×mean (accum is a 3-frame SUM).
   - **vec_small's real purpose/limits**: for |v| below ~2^(N_STAGE) the CORDIC's successive-shift stages underflow and the angle resolution degrades (≈atan(2^−log2|v|)); the empirical scan showed T ≤ 500 is safe, T ≥ 1000 is not, on x16.

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - **The array geometry lives in exactly two constants** in `tdoa_solver.sv`: `COS_K` and `TAU_MAX`. `mic_coord_x/y` are **dead ports** — propagated top → phat_position_top → tdoa_solver but never referenced in the solver body (only declared at lines 40-41). Changing them alone changes nothing functionally; the synthesizer optimizes them away.
   - **The COS_K calibration formula**: `cos_Q23 = (τ_Q10 × COS_K) >>> shift`, where the ideal is `K = c/(2d·fs)/1024 × 2^23` with `c = 34300 cm/s`, `fs = 10000 Hz`, `2d` = opposite-mic baseline in cm. Equivalently `K_eff = 28098.56/(2d_cm)` and `TAU_MAX = 2d_cm × 298.542`.
     - Old (2d = 40 cm): K = 702.464, `COS_K = 1405`, `>>>1` → K_eff = 702.5 (+51 ppm), `TAU_MAX = 11941`.
     - New (2d = 15 cm): K = 1873.237, `COS_K = 7493`, `>>>2` → K_eff = 1873.25 (**+6.8 ppm**), `TAU_MAX = 4478`.
     - Note: `(τ×COS_K)>>>n` equals `floor(τ×COS_K/2^n)`, NOT `τ×(COS_K>>n)` — so `1405>>>1` really does give 702.5 on average. Verified.
   - **`K_eff = COS_K/2` with `>>>1` can only hit 936.5 or 937** for a 30 cm array; using `>>>2` gives a finer grid, which is why 7493/`>>>2` was chosen.
   - **Width safety verified for the 15 cm design**: `ca` is `reg signed [23:0]` (Q23); ca_max = 8,388,413 < 2^23−1 = 8,388,607; ca_min = −8,388,414 > −2^23; `cord_re <= {ca[23], ca[23:1]}` (an arithmetic right shift, not a left shift) so ≤ 4,194,206; `ca²` max = 70,365,472,658,569 < 2^46 = 70,368,744,177,664, so the `sum_ovf` (Σcos² > 2^46) check remains valid.
   - **The `energy_x3` naming trap** in `freq_noise_mask.sv`: `wire [25:0] energy_x3 = {energy, 2'b00} + {2'b00, energy};` is `4E + E = 5E`, not 3E. Combined with `accum_x2[26:1] = accum` (= 3·mean), `pass = 5E > accum` ⟺ `E > 0.6×mean`. This matches doc/08 §1's "5E>accum" description. doc/07's 改动② wants `3E > 2×accum` ⟺ `E > 2×mean`.
   - **The band gate's proven benefit** (doc/08 §6.7, x16 material = in-band drone + φ=180° out-of-band interferer ~10 dB stronger): |τ23| mean/max **1490/10

Intent:
- Diagnose why the FPGA GCC-PHAT direction-finding system works well within 5m but gives no detection beyond 5m.
- Optimize the design so it can detect/measure drone targets out to 50m.
- Use the supplied real recordings to validate the diagnosis and fixes.
- Earlier request in the original session: "先熟读doc文档" (thoroughly read all documentation in `doc/`), which was completed.

## 2. Key Technical Concepts

- FPGA GCC-PHAT sound-source direction finding: 4-mic cross array, 24-bit I2S mics, 10 kHz, 256-point frames, Hann window, 1024-point forward FFT with 256→1024 zero-padding, band-pass mask bins 26–127 (1.016–4.961 kHz of the 256-point FFT), PHAT normalization, 6-pair cross-spectrum, 1024-point IFFT, peak search in ±45 IFFT points, parabolic interpolation, TDOA Q10, direction cosines Q23, CORDIC, azimuth/elevation 0.1°, UART 91-byte frames.
- Front-end: 4th-order Butterworth 900 Hz high-pass (`highpass_900`) plus fixed gain ×16 (`HPF_ENABLE=1`, `HPF_GAIN_SHIFT=4`) before frame buffer.
- `freq_noise_mask`: learns 3 frames of ch0 energy; pass if `8E > accum` (E > 0.375×mean); only bins 26–127.
- `tdoa_solver`: 128-frame sliding-window, 6-pair joint histogram vote over 23 bins (±11 samples), search every 16 frames, 3-point median; `direction_valid = vote_ready && conf_ok`; confidence `best_score > filled`; first 128 frames force V=0; 5-frame median fallback before window fills.
- `COS_K = 46588 >>> 6` (K_eff=727.9375) for 2d=38.6 cm (radius 19.3 cm); `TAU_MAX=11523`.
- CORDIC small-value gate `SMALL_LIM=165` = R_MIN(100)×K(1.6468); bins with |v|<100 zeroed (phase output zero).
- FFT fixed-point scaling: forward 1024-point zero-padded FFT uses scale schedule `10'b10_10_10_10_01` (total 2^-9); IFFT uses 11 bits (2^-11). Complex multiply shifts right 24 bits.
- `complex_mult_24b`: 24×24 products, sum, `sat(v) = v >>> 24`, 3-stage pipeline.
- `window_oper_24b`: `out = data × win / 2^23` (Q23 Hann).
- FFT mux: each channel gets 256 data + 768 zeros; only bins k=4b (b=0..128) stored; mask uses ch0 spectrum.
- Frame buffer: ping-pong BRAM, writes on `ch_valid[0]`, read selects opposite buffer.
- Real-data analysis metrics: per-frame band RMS, single-frame magnitude-squared coherence over 102 bins, GCC-PHAT peak lag, coherence-weighted phase-slope regression, global phase grid fit, RTL chain replay.
- Pure-Python DSP (no numpy/scipy available): radix-2 FFT/IFFT, Hann, fixed-point HPF replica, coherence, GCC-PHAT.
- Narrowband/tonal ambiguity: cross-correlation peaks spaced ~4 samples (≈2.5 kHz), causing aliased TDOA estimates; resolved by global phase fit across all 102 bins.
- Geometry relations for cross array: τ01 ∝ u, τ23 ∝ v, τ02=(τ01−τ23)/2, τ13=−τ02, τ03=(τ01+τ23)/2, τ12=−τ03.
- AGC concept: shared gain across all 4 channels (to preserve TDOA), power-of-two gain shift, level detection per 256-sample frame, mask re-learning on gain change.

All docs in `D:\vivado_project\FPGA\GCC-PHAT\GCC-PHAT\doc\`: `00_交付说明.md`, `01_定点误差分析报告.md`, `02_接口时序说明.md`, `03_仿真验证记录.md`, `04_板级调试指南.md`, `05_工程重建指南.md`, `06_升级8麦克风_48kHz指南.md`, `07_特定频率无人机识别调参指南.md`, `08_真实录音仿真与掩码配置对比.md`.

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - 4× parallel 256-point forward FFT (native, no zero-padding) replacing one 1024-point TDM FFT.
   - Mathematical equivalence: 1024-point zero-padded FFT bins at k=4b equal native 256-point FFT bin b; both use total scaling 2^-9.
   - Xilinx FFT v9.1 IP `xfft_256`: pipelined streaming I/O, 24-bit, scaled, natural order, block RAM, realtime throttle, runtime-configurable NFFT=8.
   - 256-point config word layout from demo TB: NFFT [3:0], FWD_INV [8], SCALE_SCH [16:9] (8 bits). CFG_WORD = `{7'b0, 8'b10101011, 1'b1, 4'b0, 4'd8}` = 24'h015708.
   - 1024-point IFFT retained (`xfft_0`) for 4× time interpolation; IFFT scale schedule via `get_scale_sch(10)` = total 2^-11.

----- [d--vivado-project-FPGA-GCC-PHAT-GCC-PHAT/3bec8b23] 2026-10-30T08:30:37.180Z
This session is being continued from a previous conversation that ran out of context. The summary below covers the earlier portion of the conversation.

<tools-used-before-summary>Bash, Edit, Glob, Grep, Read, TaskStop, ToolSearch, Write</tools-used-before-summary>
Summary:
1. Primary Request and Intent:
   - **Most recent explicit requests (verbatim):**
     - “补充一点，输出云台控制的pwm波形用b13和e14两个引脚” — add gimbal-control PWM outputs on pins **B13** and **E14**.
     - “需求：二维云台角度映射逻辑 / 硬件组成 / 使用两个 SG90 舵机搭建二自由度云台。一号舵机负责方位角（方向角），二号舵机负责俯仰角。/ 舵机行程限制 / 一号舵机的有效输入范围是 0 到 180 度。二号舵机受机械结构限制，有效输入范围是 10 到 180 度，下限不是 0。/ 云台输入 / 云台接收两个角度：方位角和俯仰角。方位角范围为负 180 度到正 180 度，正方向表示云台朝前，负方向表示云台朝后。/ 核心转换规则 / 由于舵机只能接收 0 到 180 度，当方位角落在负 180 度到 0 度之间时，云台需要整体翻转 180 度，把负方向的角度换算到正向行程内。具体分两种情况。/ 当方位角大于等于 0 时，属于正向，不翻转。一号舵机输出等于方位角本身，二号舵机输出等于俯仰角本身。/ 当方位角小于 0 时，属于负向，需要翻转。一号舵机输出等于方位角加 180，把负角度平移进 0 到 180 度区间。二号舵机输出等于 180 减俯仰角，即俯仰角取补角。/ 示例验证 / 输入方位角负 150 度、俯仰角 30 度时，方位角小于 0，触发翻转。一号舵机输出为负 150 加 180，等于 30 度。二号舵机输出为 180 减 30，等于 150 度。最终输出（30, 150），与预期一致。/ 边界与异常处理 / 二号舵机下限为 10 度，因此当翻转后计算出的俯仰角低于 10 度时，会出现超限。”
   - **Previous request (completed):** organize all files in the project folder — “整理该文件夹下的所有文件”.
   - **Earlier completed request:** 4-way parallel FFT instead of TDM, delete 8-channel/48kHz reserves, spend resources for accuracy/speed.
   - **Security/process constraints in effect (preserve verbatim):** `python` is a Microsoft Store placeholder (exit 49); always use `python3.11 -I`. No git repository; no commits/PRs.

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - XC7A100T-FGG484-2, Vivado 2018.3, 50 MHz single clock; 4-mic cross array 2d=38.6 cm; I2S 10 kHz; 256-point frames; 4× parallel `xfft_256`; 1024-point IFFT `xfft_0`; PHAT/CORDIC; 128-frame vote `SEARCH_PER=4`; UART 115200 91-byte frames; 8-digit 74HC595 display.
   - Gimbal: SG90 ×2; servo1 azimuth 0–180°; servo2 elevation mechanical range 10–180°; flip mapping at az<0; standard hobby PWM 50 Hz (20 ms), 0.5–2.5 ms ↔ 0–180°, 1 µs resolution.
   - Angle units 0.1°: `target_azimuth_01deg` signed 16-bit ∈ [−1800,+1800]; `target_elevation_01deg` signed 16-bit ∈ [0,+900] (project actual); `direction_valid` is a **single-cycle pulse per frame** (seg8 comment: 39 Hz), while phat_position_top holds target angle registers when `dir_valid_gated=0` — so hold-last is enforced upstream.
   - Vivado pin query: `get_package_pins` requires an open/elaborated design; used `open_checkpoint` on `GCC-PHAT.runs/synth_1/top_gcc_phat.dcp`; package_pin properties are `BANK`, `PIN_FUNC` (not `IO_TYPE`). Passing six-distance mem definitively uses `--highpass 900` (4th-order Butterworth).

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - FPGA GCC-PHAT direction finding, XC7A100T-FGG484-2, Vivado 2018.3 / xsim, single 50 MHz clock, 10 kHz sample rate, 256-point frames, 4-mic cross array radius 20 cm.
   - 4th-order Butterworth high-pass fc = 900 Hz @ fs = 10 kHz = two cascaded 2nd-order sections (Q = 0.54119610 / 1.30656296), DF-I direct form.
   - Q1.17 fixed-point coefficients: S1 = (b0 80847, b1 −161695, b2 80847, a1 −148047, a2 44271); S2 = (b0 100303, b1 −200605, b2 100303, a1 −183673, a2 86465). Recursion: `acc = b0·x + b1·x1 + b2·x2 − a1·y1 − a2·y2` (a1/a2 already negated per standard DF-I).
   - Widths: COEF_W=20, MID_W=26 (intermediate saturation), ACC_W=48, PROD_W=46, output DATA_WIDTH=24.
   - Symmetric rounding (round half away from zero) implemented as negate → add 2^16 → arithmetic shift 17 → negate, because `(acc − 2^16) >>> 17` mis-rounds exact negative multiples of 2^17.
   - **Pipeline constraint: adjacent `din_valid` pulses must be ≥2 clocks apart** (stage-1 output is written back to the recursion state one cycle later). Board I2S spacing is ~1875 clocks — ample.
   - CORDIC small-value gate: `SMALL_LIM = 28'sd165` in `cordic_phat_norm_24b.sv` (= R_MIN 100 × K 1.64676); bins with `|v| < 165` have their phase zeroed. `v = (|X_a|/512)·(|X_b|/512)/2^24`.
   - FAST_SIM uses `sim_audio/fast_pkg/project_global_pkg.sv` with CUR_SAMPLE_FREQ=100 kHz, UART_BAUD_DIV=43.
   - Tooling: `/d/vivado/Vivado/2018.3/bin/{xvlog,xelab,xsim,vivado}`; Python must be `python3.11 -I` (`/c/msys64/ucrt64/bin/python3.11`) because `python` is a Microsoft Store stub returning exit 49.

```systemverilog
module highpass_900 #(
    parameter integer GAIN_SHIFT = 4,
    parameter integer ENABLE     = 1
)(
    input  wire                          clk_50m,
    input  wire                          rst_n,
    input  wire signed [DATA_WIDTH-1:0]  din,
    input  wire                          din_valid,
    output wire signed [DATA_WIDTH-1:0]  dout,
    output wire                          dout_valid
);
    localparam integer COEF_FRAC = 17;
    localparam integer COEF_W    = 20;
    localparam integer MID_W     = 26;
    localparam integer ACC_W     = 48;
    localparam integer PROD_W    = COEF_W + MID_W;  // 46
    localparam signed [COEF_W-1:0] S1_B0 =  20'sd80847;  // etc for all 10
    localparam signed [ACC_W-1:0] RND_HALF = 48'sd65536;
    function automatic signed [ACC_W-1:0] rnd_q17(input signed [ACC_W-1:0] acc);
        if (acc < 0) rnd_q17 = -((-acc + RND_HALF) >>> COEF_FRAC);
        else         rnd_q17 =  ( (acc  + RND_HALF) >>> COEF_FRAC);
    endfunction
    function automatic signed [MID_W-1:0] sat_mid(input signed [ACC_W-1:0] v);
        if      (v > 26'sd33554431) sat_mid = 26'sh1FFFFFF;
        else if (v < 26'sh2000000)  sat_mid = 26'sh2000000;   // -2^25
        else

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - FPGA GCC-PHAT direction finding, XC7A100T-FGG484-2, Vivado 2018.3, single 50 MHz clock, 10 kHz I2S sampling, 256-point frames, 4-mic cross array radius 20 cm (2d = 40 cm).
   - 74HC595 serial display protocol: 16-bit frame MSB-first `{DP, seg[6:0], sel[7:0]}`, SHCP = 25 MHz (50 MHz ÷ 2), 33-state machine × 2 clocks = 66 cycles/frame (~1.32 µs), STCP latch pulse, 1 kHz digit scan (8 ms full frame, 125 Hz refresh).
   - Segment encoding active-low `seg[6:0] = {g,f,e,d,c,b,a}`; `sel` active-high one-hot, bit0 = rightmost digit (assumed, from reference `hex8.v` where `disp_data[3:0]` → `sel=8'b0000_0001`).
   - `direction_valid` from `tdoa_solver` is a **single-cycle pulse** per frame (39 Hz) — must be converted to a level for display, hence a 22-bit hold/timeout counter (~84 ms).
   - BCD conversion of 0..999 via constant division on 10 bits (`/100`, `%10`).
   - Report format: `run_sim_all.sh` counts PASS/FAIL lines; `xelab -sv --timescale 1ns/1ps --debug typical --relax`.
   - Tooling: `/d/vivado/Vivado/2018.3/bin/{xvlog,xelab,xsim,vivado}`; Python must be `/c/msys64/ucrt64/bin/python3.11 -I` (bare `python` is a Microsoft Store stub returning 49).

3. Files and Code Sections:

   - **`GCC-PHAT.srcs/sources_1/new/seg8_hc595.sv`** (NEW — core deliverable). Full module with parameters `SCAN_DIV = 50_000`, `DP_BIT = 1'b0`. Structure:
```systemverilog
    reg [15:0] scan_cnt;
    reg [2:0]  dig_idx;
    wire       scan_tick = (scan_cnt == SCAN_DIV - 1);
    // 10-bit BCD split
    wire [15:0] v_clamp = (value > 16'd999) ? 16'd999 : value;
    wire [9:0]  v10     = v_clamp[9:0];
    wire [3:0]  d_hun   = v10 / 10'd100;
    wire [9:0]  rem100  = v10 - d_hun * 10'd100;
    wire [3:0]  d_ten   = rem100 / 4'd10;
    wire [3:0]  d_one   = rem100 - d_ten * 4'd10;
    localparam [3:0] NIB_BLANK = 4'hF;
    localparam [3:0] NIB_DASH  = 4'hE;
    // nib: !valid -> DASH; dig0 -> d_one, dig1 -> d_ten, dig2 -> d_hun,
    //      dig3 -> 4'h0, default -> BLANK
    // seg decode: 0:7'b1000000 ... 9:7'b0010000, DASH:7'b0111111, default:7'b1111111
    reg [7:0] sel;  always @(*) sel = 8'b0000_0001 << dig_idx;
    reg       div_cnt; wire sck_plus = (div_cnt == 1'b1);
    reg [5:0] edge_cnt;   // 0..32
    reg [15:0] frame;
    // 复位值 = 段全灭 + 位选全 0（整屏熄灭）
    if (!rst_n) frame <= {DP_BIT, 7'b1111111, 8'b0000_0000};
    else if (sck_plus && (edge_cnt == 6'd32)) frame <= {DP_BIT, seg, sel};
    // case(edge_cnt): 0 -> ds=frame[15];

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - FPGA GCC-PHAT direction finding, XC7A100T-FGG484-2, Vivado 2018.3 / xsim, single 50 MHz clock, 10 kHz sample rate, 256-point frames, 4-mic cross array radius 20 cm.
   - 4th-order Butterworth high-pass fc = 900 Hz @ fs = 10 kHz = two cascaded 2nd-order sections (Q = 0.54119610 / 1.30656296), DF-I direct form.
   - Q1.17 fixed-point coefficients: S1 = (b0 80847, b1 −161695, b2 80847, a1 −148047, a2 44271); S2 = (b0 100303, b1 −200605, b2 100303, a1 −183673, a2 86465). Recursion: `acc = b0·x + b1·x1 + b2·x2 − a1·y1 − a2·y2` (a1/a2 already negated per standard DF-I).
   - Widths: COEF_W=20, MID_W=26 (intermediate saturation), ACC_W=48, PROD_W=46, output DATA_WIDTH=24.
   - Symmetric rounding (round half away from zero) implemented as negate → add 2^16 → arithmetic shift 17 → negate, because `(acc − 2^16) >>> 17` mis-rounds exact negative multiples of 2^17.
   - **Pipeline constraint: adjacent `din_valid` pulses must be ≥2 clocks apart** (stage-1 output is written back to the recursion state one cycle later). Board I2S spacing is ~1875 clocks — ample.
   - CORDIC small-value gate: `SMALL_LIM = 28'sd165` in `cordic_phat_norm_24b.sv` (= R_MIN 100 × K 1.64676); bins with `|v| < 165` have their phase zeroed. `v = (|X_a|/512)·(|X_b|/512)/2^24`.
   - FAST_SIM uses `sim_audio/fast_pkg/project_global_pkg.sv` with CUR_SAMPLE_FREQ=100 kHz, UART_BAUD_DIV=43.
   - Tooling: `/d/vivado/Vivado/2018.3/bin/{xvlog,xelab,xsim,vivado}`; Python must be `python3.11 -I` (`/c/msys64/ucrt64/bin/python3.11`) because `python` is a Microsoft Store stub returning exit 49.

   - **`GCC-PHAT.srcs/sources_1/new/highpass_900.sv`** (NEW, ~200 lines) — the core deliverable. Key structure:

```systemverilog
    reg [15:0] scan_cnt;
    reg [2:0]  dig_idx;
    wire       scan_tick = (scan_cnt == SCAN_DIV - 1);
    // 10-bit BCD split
    wire [15:0] v_clamp = (value > 16'd999) ? 16'd999 : value;
    wire [9:0]  v10     = v_clamp[9:0];
    wire [3:0]  d_hun   = v10 / 10'd100;
    wire [9:0]  rem100  = v10 - d_hun * 10'd100;
    wire [3:0]  d_ten   = rem100 / 4'd10;
    wire [3:0]  d_one   = rem100 - d_ten * 4'd10;
    localparam [3:0] NIB_BLANK = 4'hF;
    localparam [3:0] NIB_DASH  = 4'hE;
    // nib: !valid -> DASH; dig0 -> d_one, dig1 -> d_ten, dig2 -> d_hun,
    //      dig3 -> 4'h0, default -> BLANK
    // seg decode: 0:7'b1000000 ... 9:7'b0010000, DASH:7'b0111111, default:7'b1111111
    reg [7:0] sel;  always @(*) sel = 8'b0000_0001 << dig_idx;
    reg       div_cnt; wire sck_plus = (div_cnt == 1'b1);
    reg [5:0] edge_cnt;   // 0..32
    reg [15:0] frame;
    // 复位值 = 段全灭 + 位选全 0（整屏熄灭）
    if (!rst_n) frame <= {DP_BIT, 7'b1111111, 8'b0000_0000};
    else if (sck_plus && (edge_cnt == 6'd32)) frame <= {DP_BIT, seg, sel};
    // case(edge_cnt): 0 -> ds=frame[15];

   - PCM 定点：能量 `E = re_sq[46:23] + im_sq[46:23]`；5E 由 `{energy,2'b00}+{2'b00,energy}` 实现；比较 `accum_x2[26:1]` 即 accum。

   - User then said “开始吧” — authorizing implementation of changes 1+2+3 I proposed:
     1. Constrain the IFFT peak search to the physical TDOA window (±11.25 samples / ±45 interpolated points) and clamp τ to ±11523.
     2. On Σ=cos²α+cos²β>1, do not reject the whole frame: clamp cosγ=0, ρ=1, output elevation 0° and V=1, preserving azimuth.
     3. Change the mask threshold from 5E to 8E and add a 5-frame median filter on τ01/τ23 before solving.
   - Constraint: after code changes, do NOT run simulations — only syntax-check with xvlog.

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - FPGA GCC-PHAT direction finding: XC7A100T-FGG484, 50 MHz, 4-mic cross array, radius 19.3 cm (2d=38.6 cm), 10 kHz I2S 24-bit input, 256-point FFT, 1024-point IFFT (4× time interpolation), 6 mic pairs.
   - Geometry: τ_max = 2d·fs/c = 11.2536 samples; COS_K=46588 (K_eff=727.9375 after >>>6); TAU_MAX=11523 Q10; cosα = τ·46588>>>6 (Q23).
   - Elevation conditioning: θ = atan2(√(1−Σ),√Σ); dθ/dτ = 1/(τ_max·sinθ) ≈ 11.4°/sample at θ=26.6°, ~26°/sample at 10 m/2 m; azimuth φ=atan2(cb,ca) is much less sensitive to τ01 when the source is along x (≈5.7°/sample via τ23). This explains stable azimuth + jumping elevation.
   - Distance failure: as θ→0, τ01→τ_max and margin τ_max(1−cosθ) shrinks as θ² (4 m/2 m: 1.19 samples; 8 m: 0.34; 10 m: 0.22; 20 m: 0.06). τ noise pushes estimates over TAU_MAX → `cos_bad`; Σ→1 → `sum_ovf`. Both reject the entire frame, so both angles vanish (frame-level `direction_valid` gate).
   - Height failure: θ increases, Σ decreases, so geometry gate should not fire; if it still fails, suspect mask learning/level/mic directivity/source spectrum.
   - Mask learning: first 3 frames (~77 ms) sum ch0 energy into `accum` (26-bit); application condition previously `5E > accum` ⇔ E > 0.6× learned average (only 2.2 dB margin, distance ×1.29); 8E ⇔ 0.375× average (4.3 dB, ×1.63); 10E would be 0.3× (5.2 dB, ×1.83). Mask is per-frame but baseline is fixed at boot; if the source is present during learning, moving it (or drone RPM change) invalidates the mask.
   - UART frame: 91 bytes; T01..T23 fields are `|τ|>>10` integer samples (three digits, sign); sent every frame even when V=0. `phat_position_top` clears tx_az/tx_el to 0 when V=0, so A/E read 0.0 on invalid frames; 7-seg holds the last valid angle and shows “-” only before the first valid.
   - 5-element median sorting network: compare-swap pairs (0,1)(3,4)(2,4)(2,3)(1,4)(0,3)(0,2)(1,3)(1,2); verified in Python over all 120 permutations.
   - Fundamental limits: planar array elevation sensitivity ∝ sinθ→0 at the horizon; larger baseline improves absolute angle noise and gate margin; a 3D array with a vertical baseline has τ_v∝sinθ and maximum dτ/dθ at θ=0 — the real fix for low elevation.

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - FPGA GCC-PHAT: 4-mic cross array, radius R=19.3 cm, 2d=38.6 cm; τ_max = 2d·fs/c = 11.2536 samples; COS_K=46588 (K_eff=727.9375 after >>>6); TAU_MAX=11523 Q10.
   - Planar-array elevation singularity: τ = τ_max·cosθ·cosφ; ∂τ/∂θ = τ_max·sinθ·cosφ → 0 at the horizon. Sensitivity numbers: 0.088 samples/deg at 4 m/2 m (26.6°), 0.039 at 10 m/2 m (11.3°), 0.020 at 20 m/2 m (5.7°).
   - Vertical baseline sensitivity: ∂τ/∂θ = (h·fs/c)·cosθ; noise formula σ_θ(°) ≈ 1.97·σ_τ/h(m) for one pair. h=25 mm gives ~8° at σ_τ=0.1 (12–24° at σ_τ=0.3); h=100 mm ~2°; h=200 mm ~1°; h=300 mm ~0.65°. The 8-mic two-layer array improves only ~2× over one pair due to correlated noise, not √16.
   - 3D options evaluated: A) cross + 5th mic above center (h=30–40 cm), 10 pairs; B) 4-mic pyramid/tetrahedron; C) 6-mic octahedron (±R on x/y/z), 15 pairs; D) rotate cross 90° (38.6 cm vertical baseline but loses 360° azimuth). User rejected all hardware changes.
   - Firmware-only improvement candidates: (1) 3-frame mask majority vote; (2) elevation-specific heavier smoothing (9-frame median or slow IIR) with azimuth kept at 5-frame; (3) use the 4 idle diagonal pair delays in a 2×2 least-squares/consistency check; (4) mask energy from all 4 channels instead of ch0; (5) known-angle calibration of 6 fixed pair offsets.
   - Diagonal pair identities (with the RTL sign convention): τ03+τ12≈0, τ02+τ13≈0, τ03−τ12≈τ01+τ23, τ02−τ13≈τ01−τ23. Near-field curvature at 4 m introduces ~0.1–0.3 sample residual; thresholds must allow it.
   - UART frame: 91 bytes; T01..T23 fields are `|τ|>>10` integer samples (three digits, sign); frames sent every frame even when V=0; `phat_position_top` clears tx_az/tx_el to 0 when V=0; 7-seg holds last valid angle.
   - Vivado build flow: `reset_run synth_1` → launch → `reset_run impl_1` → `launch_runs impl_1 -to_step write_bitstream`; set `STEPS.WRITE_BITSTREAM.ARGS.BIN_FILE true` to produce both .bit and .bin.

   - I2S clocking: `clk_gen.sv` uses a fractional accumulator, BCLK=64×CUR_SAMPLE_FREQ, LRCK toggles every 32 BCLK falling edges → LRCK=CUR_SAMPLE_FREQ=10 kHz exactly (FPGA is I2S master).

Invalid frames therefore send A:000.0/E:000.0 with V:0. The 7-seg target_azimuth/elevation only update on dir_valid_gated, so the display holds the last valid.
   - Proposed firmware algorithm: exact 128-frame sliding histogram per pair (6 pairs × 23 bins, 1 sample/bin), ring buffer of bin indices; per frame decrement oldest/increment newest; every 16 frames search 23×23 candidate (τ01,τ23) maximizing the six-pair joint score; half-bin interpolated lookup for derived delays; parabolic sub-sample interpolation; median-of-3 output; confidence gate for V; med5 fallback during warm-up.
   - Half-bin scoring: candidate (a,b) integers; d=(a−b)/2 (τ02), e=(a+b−22)/2 (τ03). If parity even, use that exact bin; if odd, sum the two adjacent bins. Score is 2× so weights stay equal. Background score2 = 12·filled/23; confidence condition `best_score2 > filled` ≈ ratio 1.917.
   - Bin mapping for RTL raw Q10: BIN_CENTER_OFF=11776 Q10; bin=clamp((τ_Q10+11776)>>>10, 0, 22); bin center = (a−11) samples. This avoids the ±0.25/±0.5 sample center bias of the naive mapping and the truncation pile-up at 0 present in the log.

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - Vivado 2018.3，XC7A100T-FGG484-2，单时钟域 50 MHz。
   - 4 路十字麦克风阵列，臂长 ±20 cm，对臂间距 2d=38.6 cm（实测），10 kHz I2S 采样，256 点/帧，39.06 帧/s。
   - 算法链：Hanning 加窗 → 4 颗 256 点 FFT 并行 → 频点噪声掩码 → PHAT → 6 对互谱 1024 点 IFFT（补零内插）→ 抛物线峰值检测 → 正交基线方向余弦测向 → 128 帧六对联合直方图投票（每 4 帧搜索一次）。
   - 双角度通道：投票角度 `target_azimuth_01deg/target_elevation_01deg/direction_valid` 供 UART/数码管；快速角度 `fast_azimuth_01deg/fast_elevation_01deg/fast_valid`（01/23 两对 5 帧中值，每帧更新）供云台 PWM。
   - UART 115200 8N1，91 字节 ASCII 帧；74HC595 8 位数码管；SG90 双路 50 Hz PWM，1.0–2.0 ms。
   - 定点格式：cosα/cosβ Q23，Σcos² Q46，TDOA Q10（1 LSB=1/1024 采样点）；`COS_K=46588`，`TAU_MAX=11523`。
   - AGC（0..+48 dB，6 dB 升挡迟滞）、HPF、固定带通掩码 `MASK_BIN_LO=26` / `MASK_BIN_HI=127`、`SMALL_LIM=165`。
   - 零时延误锁：PHAT 在无相干声源/低信噪比时互谱接近随机相位，128 帧直方图在 0 时延档堆积，投票输出 θ≈90°、方位角无意义；`best_score > filled` 置信判据可能被误导。
   - 数码管“保持上一刻”语义：只在 `direction_valid` 单周期脉冲时锁存，之后无新有效帧则一直显示旧值。
   - 工具：`tools/vote_sim.py`（128 帧、search=4 回放）、`record_capture.py`、`uart_monitor.py`、`analysis_far/out/*.txt`。

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - Vivado 2018.3, XC7A100T-FGG484-2, single 50 MHz clock domain.
   - 4-microphone cross array, arm half-length ±20 cm, measured opposite-arm spacing 2d = 38.6 cm (docs still say 40 cm in places); 10 kHz I2S sampling, 256-point frames, 39.06 frames/s.
   - Algorithm chain: Hanning window → 4 parallel 256-point FFT → frequency-bin noise mask → PHAT → six-pair 1024-point IFFT (zero-padded interpolation) → parabolic peak detection → orthogonal-baseline direction cosines → 128-frame six-pair joint histogram vote (search every 4 frames).
   - Two angle channels:
     - Vote channel: `target_azimuth_01deg`, `target_elevation_01deg`, `direction_valid` → UART/display.
     - Fast channel: `fast_azimuth_01deg`, `fast_elevation_01deg`, `fast_valid` → gimbal PWM; based on 5-frame medians of pairs 01/23, updated every frame.
   - UART 115200 8N1, 91-byte ASCII frame; 74HC595 8-digit 7-segment display; SG90 ×2 50 Hz PWM, 1.0–2.0 ms.
   - Fixed-point formats: cos Q23, Σcos² Q46, TDOA Q10; `COS_K = 32'sd46588` (K_eff = 727.9375), `TAU_MAX = 16'sd11523`; `SUM_LIMIT_Q46 = 48'h500000000000` (1.25 × 2^46).
   - Vote confidence: `conf_ok = (best_score > {4'b0, filled})`; `joint_score2` weights free pairs 01/23 by 2× and interpolates half-bins for the other four pairs; background ≈ 12·filled/23.
   - Display hold semantics: `seg8_hc595` latches only on a single-cycle `sample_valid`; no timeout, so it keeps the last valid value indefinitely.
   - Fast-path two-pass FSM: `pass=0` vote angle, `pass=1` fast angle; shares sqrt/CORDIC; ~164 cycles/frame vs. 25.6 ms frame interval.
   - GIMBAL mapping: az ≥ 0 → (servo1=az, servo2=el); az < 0 → (servo1=az+1800, servo2=1800−el). Mechanical limits servo1 [0°,180°], servo2 [10°,180°]. PWM 1000–2000 µs, 20 ms period, reset 1.5 ms.
   - Pins: `gimbal_pwm_az = B13`, `gimbal_pwm_el = E14`, both Bank 16, LVCMOS33, VCCO 3.3 V.
   - Capture format: `$GCCP,<seq>,T01:%+04d,...,A:%+06.1f,E:%+06.1f,V:%d*XX\r\n`; the T fields are integer samples (UART-formatted from Q10), not raw Q10.
